# DeepBench v5.1 — Session Log & How to Start

> Last updated: 2026-06-07
> Google Drive retired as source of truth. GitHub is single master.

---

## The Two-Window Workflow

| Window | Tool | Reads | Does |
|--------|------|-------|------|
| Design + Planning | Claude.ai | GitHub docs via raw fetch | Writes kickoff docs, reviews mocks, design decisions |
| Coding | Claude Code | GitHub `CLAUDE.md` + `docs/` auto-read | Writes code, runs tests, commits to dev |

**You never write kickoff docs manually.** Ask Claude.ai to generate one. It
fetches GitHub docs and produces a fully self-contained kickoff doc rendered
as an **artifact panel** (copy icon accessible) — never as a download or raw
markdown. Paste it into Claude Code to start the session.

---

## How to Start a Coding Session

### Step 1 — Get the kickoff doc from Claude.ai

Open Claude.ai (DeepBench project) and say:

> "Generate the kickoff doc for [session name or feature ID]."

Claude.ai will fetch `CLAUDE.md`, `FEATURES.md`, `SESSIONS.md`, and
`STANDARDS.md` from GitHub, confirm current version and blockers, and
render the kickoff doc as an artifact panel.

### Step 2 — Run Claude Code

```
cd C:\Projects\deepbench-frontend
claude
```

### Step 3 — Paste the kickoff doc

Paste the kickoff doc. Claude Code reads `CLAUDE.md` automatically before
processing — it is already oriented. The kickoff doc tells it what to build.

### Step 4 — Claude Code executes

1. Read `CLAUDE.md` (automatic)
2. Read any additional files listed in kickoff doc Step 0
3. Run the Node.js test — ALL PASS required
4. Write the code
5. Run `npm run build` — zero errors required
6. Commit and push to `dev`
7. Update version state in `CLAUDE.md` Section 12 (automatic)
8. Report back with verification checklist and browser test results

### Step 5 — You run Manual QA

Open the dev URL, run the Manual QA checklist, report PASS/FAIL back to
Claude Code.

### Step 6 — Session closes

When all QA items pass, say "Close [FEATURE-ID]" to Claude Code.
Claude Code marks it Done in `FEATURES.md` and commits.

---

## How to Start a UX Review Session

UX review sessions happen in Claude.ai only — no Claude Code involved.

Open Claude.ai (DeepBench project) and say:

> "Starting UX review for [screen]. Generate the kickoff doc when ready."

Claude.ai fetches GitHub docs, asks you to describe or share a screenshot
of what you want changed, produces a mock or layout description for approval,
then writes the kickoff doc for the coding session that implements the changes.

---

## Kickoff Doc — Required Sections (SUPERSEDED — see `docs/STANDARDS.md` §3)

> This v5.1-era 10-section list is retired. The canonical, enumerated kickoff structure is `docs/STANDARDS.md` §3 (11 sections). Kept below only as history — do not use it.

1. **SESSION** — name, version, branch, files to read in Step 0
2. **CONTEXT** — what the feature does and why
3. **STUB definitions** — e.g. `const MICHELLE = {...}` if needed
4. **TASKS** — max 4, each with exact code spec
5. **DESIGN RULES** — tokens, fonts, styling (required for UI sessions)
6. **SCOPE RULES** — what NOT to touch
7. **NODE.JS TEST** — full test code written out (not described)
8. **CLAUDE CODE VERIFICATION CHECKLIST** — from STANDARDS.md
9. **COMMIT instruction** — exact message format: `v5.1.X | FEATURE-ID | description`
10. **MANUAL QA CHECKLIST** — max 12 session-specific steps, ends with
    "Report back PASS/FAIL/NEW REQUIREMENT"

Claude Code has no Drive access, but it does have persistent cross-session
memory as of 2026-07-01 — kickoff docs no longer need to restate a *standing*
rule (23-field agent standard, AI Audit wiring, STANDARDS.md Section 5
checklist categories) in full prose; naming it is enough ("STANDARDS.md
Section 11 applies"). Session-specific facts — exact values, files, scope,
test assertions for *this* session — still must be fully spelled out.
"As discussed" is still forbidden for anything that isn't a standing rule.
See `docs/STANDARDS.md` Section 3 for the full rule.

## Architectural Decisions Log

| Decision | Session | Detail |
|----------|---------|--------|
| No hardcoded selectors | S01 | Agents learn through training |
| Client-side CSV processing | S01 | Compliance advantage |
| Single-shot LLM for most tasks | S01 | Agentic only when step N+1 needs step N output |
| Agent execution uses existing api/brief.js | S01 | Not rewritten |
| Pat runs Railway backend with skipRag=true | S03 | Same backend as Brent |
| Michelle logic lives in Supabase agent_configs | S10b | NOT in code |
| api/plan.js + api/title.js read from Supabase | S10b | Locked to S-BENCH-01 |
| mergeSteps() = single source of truth | S10a | Three named operations only |
| Three named step operations | S14p2 | initializeFromSupabase, initializeFromFirstPlan, updateFromPlan |
| saveStepsToSupabase() writes full array | S14p2 | Including archived |
| pendingArchive preserved in all writes | S14p2 | Stripped only on user approve |
| Answers on step.questions[n].a | S14p4 | Never ephemeral state |
| stepsContext strips mergeStatus/pendingArchive/title_edited | S14p4d | Before LLM call |
| task.steps = mergedToSet.active after Update Plan | S14p4c | — |
| MichelleAvatar.jsx stub | S10p | Supabase profile wiring = S-BENCH-01 |
| title_edited flag | S14 | User owns title after first edit, never overwritten |
| Update Steps button lives in StepList.jsx | S15c | NOT TaskInstructionsScreen.jsx |
| AIDiamond.jsx heartbeat component | S15a | Do not refactor without dedicated session |
| Fast-forward merge dev → main | S15 | Only after John explicit QA confirmation |
| Kickoff docs render as artifact panels | S15 | Never as downloads or raw markdown |

## Rolled-out session detail (from CLAUDE-STATE.md's 3-session rolling window)

- **S-LOG-36-design / S-LOG-36** (v6.3.134, `b808f50`, 2026-07-23, worktree `design-log-36-0723`, branch renamed in place to `session/log-36-coding`) — ✅ **Done, built and live-QA passed by the design session directly.** **Post-build addendum (the part worth reading twice):** the coding session shipped the kickoff faithfully and then reported two defects the *kickoff itself* caused. (1) **The About tile rendered `0`.** Task 4's rationale — "the hook is a cheap in-memory subscription, not a fetch, so calling it here is safe" — was true and irrelevant: `useAIActivity()` reads an in-memory `_log` that only `hydrateFromSupabase()` fills, and `git grep` across all of `src/` finds exactly **one** caller, `AIActivityPanel.jsx`'s mount effect. Open About without opening AI Audit first and the count is zero — worse than the stale 24. Fixing it honestly needs a Supabase aggregate object or a row-cap-exposed scan over 16,061 rows for one number, so on John's call the tile was **reverted** and folded into `LOG-56`, keeping the whole About panel as one screen for one conversation. The exception is pinned as a **named allowance** in the Category M regression test rather than deleted. (2) **The governed descriptions were thrown away** — the kickoff specified `select('pattern_slug, name')`, so the two researched, citation-backed patterns rendered a blank line while the *unresearched* ones still showed "Auto-detected — not yet catalogued," exactly backwards. Fixed; the vocabulary Map now carries `{name, description}`, and **two other consumers (`byService`, `patternLabelFor`) had to change with it or they'd have rendered `[object Object]`** — found by grepping every consumer before building. **Lesson John named directly:** the session asked permission to fix its own error ("why wouldn't you make that change?"). Restoring data the design already intended is not an approval-gate case; removing or altering a visual element is. **A fifth display site was also found only because John asked whether the kickoff was complete** — `useAIActivity.js:25`'s `PATTERN_NAME_BY_SLUG`, naming the By Service rows; the session had been carrying "four display areas" from the row text instead of re-deriving the list from code. Live QA (design session, against a real dev server + real Supabase): 10 rows, one flat list ordered by calls, no Structural/Reasoning/Inactive/Uncatalogued headings, no catalog-only pattern leaking in, "Patterns Logged 10" as a single number, Platform Roadmap untouched, About tile 24, Agents drawer rollup clean. Honest gaps: `Streaming` shows zero buckets (all 6 rows pre-`LOG-16` cutoff — correct, so 10 rows not the 11 predicted), the Agent Routing drawer was never exercised with real hops (same resolver, verified in the Agents drawer — inferred, not observed), and screen counts don't reconcile to raw log counts (`LOG-60`, **not** caused by this session — the counting path is byte-unchanged). New rows: `LOG-56`, `LOG-57`, `LOG-58`, `LOG-60`, `LOG-61`, `CHI-67`.
- **S-LOG-36-design — original design-phase record** (v6.3.134, 2026-07-23, worktree `design-log-36-0723`, kickoff `docs/kickoffs/v6.3.134-LOG-36-log-driven-pattern-displays.md`) — `LOG-36` (Architecture), task 2 of 7 in the `AI-35`/`LOG-23` chain. **The session's own opening frame was wrong, and the correction is the entire value of the session.** It began by treating `LOG-36` as "repoint 4 displays to a different name source," measured that only 13% of ~31,300 logged pattern tags still match `pattern_vocabulary` post-`2b`, and concluded `LOG-36` was blocked on `LOG-44`–`48`. John rejected the premise directly — *"for all displays, it should only show patterns that have logs… if the log table gets wiped out, then 0 logs and patterns will show up"* — which reverses the driver: the log enumerates what exists, the vocabulary is only a name lookup. Re-reading `ARCHITECTURE.md §19i` (which the session had until then only carried secondhand) found that **John's rule is already the LOCKED corollary, verbatim** — "read Layer A/log data as it currently exists, read Layer C as it currently exists, join them live." The defect is one line, `useAIActivity.js:62`, which seeds all 24 `PATTERN_CATALOG` entries as zero-count buckets before reading a single log row. Consequences: no blank-out (the existing `LOG-14` `humanizeSlug()` fallback covers unmatched slugs), **no dependency on the anomalies**, and each anomaly fixed later upgrades exactly one row with zero further display work. **Second correction, John-initiated:** asked to web-research the Structural/Reasoning grouping *before* adopting it into the governed vocabulary. It is invented — Anthropic's "Building Effective Agents" splits Workflows from Agents; Huang & Zhou (arXiv 2605.13850, v1 2026-03-16) use two *perpendicular* axes, Cognitive Function × Execution Topology, the latter being six "structural" archetypes and the former containing "Reasoning". DeepBench took one word from each axis and made them mutually exclusive. Split out rather than folded in: `LOG-56` (non-activity pattern displays — the Platform Roadmap section plus `AboutPanel`'s hardcoded SVG/glossary strings, two sites folded as a Tier-2 call), `LOG-57` (`src/aiPatterns.js`'s `AI_PAT`, hand-declared badges across 8 screens, live-proven to assert `react`/`browser-automation` which have **never** been logged), `LOG-58` (pick a real taxonomy — Tier 3). **A premise of John's own was also checked rather than accepted:** he expected the naming rationale to live in the activity log; `ai_activity_log` has zero content columns, but all 26 `pattern_candidates` rows carry a full `resolution` (decision, reasoning, citation) — his anomaly-session plan works, from a different table. He explicitly declined a UI to surface it and declined logging even a future item for it. File cap waived to 4 by explicit call.

POC's duplicate loop (`lib/durable-loop-poc.js`) retired per `ARCHITECTURE.md` §19b's no-duplicate-pipeline-logic rule; persistence helper relocated to `api/_lib/handlers/durable-loop.js`. Fixed the silent content-loss bug on the `delegate_to_agent` hand-off: `task` is a plain string by design, so a delegator's real structured `task_context` (citations included) never reached the delegate — Priya→Michelle→Alex returned an empty card on every hypothesis test (blocking `S-MARKET-INTEL-03`'s charts), and the same mechanism affected Marcus's Q&A display hand-off. Fix: thread the delegator's own `task_context` through `runLoop()`, merge it into the delegate's `task_context` at the `delegate_to_agent` branch (`delegation_task` key preserves the delegator's own free-text framing), plus a loud-failure guard on an empty final delegation instead of a silent hollow card. Harness-generic, 1 file (`api/capabilities/execute.js`), 2 tasks. Live-verified against the deployed dev URL — the exact Nordholm scenario now returns a full card and a real `bar_pair` chart; Marcus's Q&A path confirmed unaffected (regression check). **Corroborating evidence surfaced later the same day** (John's own live UX test, `AA-142`): even after this fix, the model can still narrate an intended hand-off in prose instead of actually calling `delegate_to_agent` — a different, upstream cause (the display-intent's own `tool_choice: 'auto'` allowing free text) that this session's fix does not address; logged as its own root-cause item, not a regression of this fix.

(2) John's follow-on, discovered mid-conversation while explaining why the display-agent pick can't be shown live: the platform's real `request_help`/`delegate_to_agent` delegation (`ARCHITECTURE.md` §19d) is genuine model reasoning, not hardcoded routing, and John wanted that fact **showcased live** as it happens, not just visible after the fact in the drawer — directly validated by re-reading `request-receivable.js`'s tool definitions and `execute.js`'s dispatch code together with him before scoping. Design: an `_onEvent` callback threaded through `runCapability()`/`runLoop()`/`resumeCapability()`/`resolveAccept()` (`api/capabilities/execute.js`) exactly like the existing `_hop_counter`/`_deadline` pattern (defaults to a no-op, zero overhead for every non-opted-in caller), firing a `delegation` event at all 5 real dispatch points — critique, `request_help`'s PM-broker call, the `delegationRequired` auto-resolve, the plain `delegate_to_agent` call, and a new `delegation_return` event when control genuinely falls back to the same agent (e.g. Owen's retry-via-Marcus; never fired for a terminal hand-off). Streamed via opt-in `stream:true` SSE, reusing `api/plan.js`'s `suggest-goal` action's already-proven `text/event-stream` idiom verbatim (`streamResult()` helper) — confirmed via direct file read this was a real, working precedent in this exact Vercel Node.js runtime, not a new capability being invented. Every non-streamed caller (`test-durable-loop-retrofit.js`, `test-s-arch-hitl-resume-01a.mjs`) gets byte-identical plain JSON. Client-side (`MarketIntelligenceScreen.jsx`): `readSSEResult()` mirrors `CreateWorkOrderScreen.jsx`'s existing reader (same idiom, not a second one); live delegation events and the pre-existing scripted macro-hop swaps both feed one shared `setStatus()`/`workingStatus`, two-tone styled (`kind: 'scripted'` vs `'orchestration'`) so a viewer can tell "this always happens" from "an agent just decided this" — the whole point of the showcase, deliberately not blurring scripted pipeline steps (Owen's mandatory review, Sam's mandatory triage) into the same visual claim as genuine agent-chosen delegation. Final receipt, John's explicit ask: `"Full Agent Routing & Answer Given in Xs"` persistent caption on the answer bubble (`msg.totalElapsedMs`, a simple end-start timestamp diff, not a running sum — mathematically identical since hops are strictly sequential with zero gaps). Also fixed 2 pre-existing `SERVICE_CATALOG` gaps found during the AI Pattern Check (`channel-intelligence` missing `Agent Delegation` despite `AA-164` already using it) and broadened the `streaming` `PATTERN_CATALOG` description to honestly cover event-granularity alongside its original token-granularity framing. **Forward-compatible with `AA-171`** (found the same night, not fixed by this session): the guardrail-block path's hardcoded `agent_id:"sam"` call bypasses the harness loop entirely (screen-scripted), so it's structurally invisible to `_onEvent` today — correctly gets only plain scripted styling until `AA-171` routes it through real agent reasoning, at which point it lands on an already-instrumented dispatch point automatically, zero new code needed. Node test 7/7 PASS (`buildSections()`'s intent-matching/null-fallback logic, `buildPatternsUsed()`'s merge/dedupe — both now-exported pure functions, no live network needed), build clean, `git diff --stat` confirmed exactly the 3 intended files, `execute.js` absent. **Design session's own live verification, not accepted from the coding report alone** (John's explicit go-ahead for one real API call, ~\$0.01, after the coding session mistakenly reported it lacked Supabase access — it actually has full access to the right project, `rallojeqnkgtxgsdsnqm`/"nigp-analyzer," same one used throughout this whole engagement): fired a real `channel-intelligence`/`ci-answer-intent` call against the dev deployment (a genuine Data Room question, "What do we know about the Japan program area?"); both the API response and the persisted `ai_activity_log` row (id 9441, created_at 19:47:42) show `patterns_used: ["structured-output","tool-use","rag"]` — `"rag"` is a value the pre-fix mechanical-only detection could never produce. Real concurrent production traffic independently confirmed the identical shape moments earlier (row 9438, 19:47:23), while a row from ~8 minutes before the deploy (9426, 19:39:59) shows the old `["structured-output","tool-use"]` only — a clean, unambiguous before/after proof, not a synthetic test fixture. John also confirmed the intended design for `AI-50c`'s reconciliation problem during this session's scoping conversation: real multi-pattern attribution (not the static per-service over-count), with each call's cost split **evenly** across however many real patterns *that specific call* exercised — so bucket sums reconcile exactly to Total Cost while still reflecting genuine per-call behavior, resolving the tension between "accurate" and "sums to the total" that a pure even-split-over-static-tags or a forced-single-primary-pattern approach couldn't cleanly resolve on their own. Second of what became 4 sessions closing out `AI-50`, found live during `AI-50b`'s own verification (content pass populating `technical_services` on 5 real Intent Skill Profiles): `execute.js`'s `logAgentTurn()` is a **second, independent write site** to `ai_activity_log` that `AI-50a`'s harness fix (`db-assembly.js` → `ai-enrichment.js` → `request-receivable.js`) never touched. ['agent-delegation'] : []), ...intent_technical_services]))`); its single call site (~line 318) now passes `intent_technical_services: enriched.intent_technical_services || []` — the field was already flowing through `enriched` since `AI-50a`, so this required no new data plumbing, only reading a value already in scope. - **S-AI-51-design/S-AI-51** (v6.2.34, `b828422`, 2026-07-14, worktree `design-ai-audit-cost-fix-0714`) — `AI-51` ✅ Done [full detail preserved in `docs/FEATURES-ARCHIVE.md`'s `AI-51` entry]. Found while John reviewed the live AI Audit screen right after `AI-50c` shipped, asking two questions: (1) why By Pattern's bucket sum ran ~\$4-6 short of Total Cost, and (2) why "Services Active" showed the impossible "21/14". Root-caused (1) as mostly-expected — calls whose intent has no `technical_services` declared yet are correctly excluded from By Pattern rather than mis-attributed, a content-coverage backlog matching `AI-50c`'s own design — **but investigating it surfaced a genuine, separate, quantified double-count bug**: `execute.js`'s `logAgentTurn()` writes a row for every turn; `sendRequest()` writes a *second* row for the same turn when terminal (non-delegating) — both carry identical tokens/`patterns_used`, describing the same real call from two logging vantage points, so both were being priced AND both counted toward `computeByPattern()`'s per-pattern totals. Live-quantified via direct Supabase queries: 1,107 paired `agent-turn` rows, \$5.58 double-counted out of \$22.82 Total Cost (~24% inflation). A blanket "zero all agent-turn cost" fix would have been wrong — a pure delegating turn has no paired wrapper row and is the sole record of that spend; confirmed this distinction matters via live pairing-rate data (`channel-intelligence` 75% paired, `data-analysis` only 12%). **Fix, 3 files:** moved `CAPABILITY_WRAPPER_TYPES`/`PAIR_WINDOW_MS` from `useAgents.js` into `useAIActivity.js` (now the canonical shared cost/pattern module; `useAgents.js` already imported `computeCallCost` from there, same import direction, no circular risk) — reusing the exact same nearby-timestamp pairing check `useAgents.js`'s `classifyRow()` already proved live for LATENCY dedup (`S-MI-20`), applied here to cost + pattern-count instead via a new exported `pairedAgentTurnIds(rows)`. Wired into both real-data cost consumers: `hydrateFromSupabase()` (zeroes cost, clears `patternsUsed` on paired rows) and `useAgents.js`'s `buildActivitySummary()` (same dedup on `d.totalCost`, `classifyRow()`'s own separate latency dedup left untouched). Also fixed `AIActivityPanel.jsx`'s "Services Active" stat hardcoding a stale `/14` literal (`SERVICE_CATALOG` has 32 real entries today) via a new `servicesCatalogTotal` field, mirroring how "Patterns Active" already works. **Coding session caught and fixed one real gap beyond the kickoff's literal spec, within the same 3 files:** `useAgentActivitySummary()`'s Supabase `.select()` was missing `id` — since `pairedAgentTurnIds()` keys its result Set by `row.id`, every row would have shared `id: undefined`, and pairing even one agent-turn row would have zeroed cost across the *entire* fetched batch, not just genuinely paired rows; added `id` to the existing query to prevent this. Node test 5/5 PASS (terminal-turn pairing, pure-delegation non-pairing, cross-agent non-pairing, outside-window non-pairing, `computeByPattern()` reconciliation), build clean, `git diff --stat` confirmed exactly the 3 intended files. **Design session's own live verification against real production data, not just the Node test:** replicated the shipped `pairedAgentTurnIds()`/cost logic directly in SQL against all 6,868 real `ai_activity_log` rows (`rallojeqnkgtxgsdsnqm`, `tenant_id='global'`) — confirmed Total Cost drops from \$23.68 to \$18.53 (removing \$5.15 across 1,027 paired rows, consistent with the pre-fix estimate), while a genuine pure-delegation `agent-turn` row with no paired wrapper (id 9746, Marcus, real tokens, `patterns_used` includes `'agent-delegation'`) correctly retains its real, non-zero cost — proving both directions of the fix on live data, not a synthetic fixture alone. Kickoff: `docs/kickoffs/v6.2.34-AI-51-agent-turn-double-count-and-service-count-fix.md`. Ran in its own worktree (`session/design-ai-audit-cost-fix-0714`).

- **S-ARCH-DURABLE-RESUME-02** (v6.2.31, `420f677`, 2026-07-14, worktree `design-qg-checkpoint-fix-0714`) — `AA-185`/`AA-187` ✅ Fixed and live-verified [full detail: `docs/FEATURES.md` `AA-185`/`AA-187` entries]. Root-caused via direct `durable_hops`/`ai_activity_log` evidence during a live John-reported question (Channel Sales Intelligence, ~2.5 min total, 16 Agent Routing rows, correct-but-costly `synthesized_as_fact` guardrail block): nested `request_help`/`delegate_to_agent` calls inherit the outer call's shrinking deadline; the harness fired `onEvent` and dispatched the nested call immediately after the model decided, with no check that enough budget remained to finish it. If the nested call checkpointed mid-flight, the outer call also checkpointed, and resume re-entered `runLoop()` from persisted state — re-asking the model for a turn it already answered (duplicate `onEvent`, `AA-185`) while the nested call's own partial work was simply abandoned, never resumed (orphaned `durable_hops` rows, `AA-187`). **Fix, 1 code file + 1 migration + 1 locked-doc correction:** `durable_hops.pending_delegation` (new jsonb column) persists the model's already-decided-but-undispatched delegation; the budget check now runs *before* `onEvent` fires and before the nested call starts (`dispatchDelegation()` extracted from the former inline `request_help`/`delegate_to_agent` blocks, parameterized so it can be called either live from `runLoop()` or from `resumeCapability()` off a persisted `pending_delegation`, no re-asking the model). `ARCHITECTURE.md` §19h's stale locked-section claim ("checkpoint-resume semantics are unaffected") corrected in place, referencing `AA-185`/`AA-187` and this session by name, per the Locked-Section-Staleness rule. Node test (`test-s-arch-durable-resume-02.mjs`, deleted before commit) 5/5 PASS against real Supabase + real Anthropic: zero `onEvent` calls before the pre-dispatch checkpoint, exactly-once dispatch on resume with no duplicate delegation events, zero orphaned nested rows, the ordinary non-checkpointing path unaffected, a genuine downstream failure (forced via a `pending_delegation` targeting a nonexistent `intent_slug`) still marks the row `failed`. Build clean, `git diff --stat` confirmed exactly `api/capabilities/execute.js` + `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`, no frontend files touched. **Live-verified against the real deployed dev preview, 3 separate isolated runs post-deployment** (2 direct API-level calls replicating `MarketIntelligenceScreen.jsx`'s exact `runIntentPipeline()`/`runQaWithQualityGate()` sequence, including a re-run of the literal original incident question; 1 full browser UI run reproducing a real guardrail-block escalation to Sam) — every run showed exactly one checkpoint/resume cycle, zero duplicate Agent Routing rows, zero orphaned `durable_hops` rows in its own isolated window, zero browser console errors, real final results (elapsed 76-91s, in the expected 35-76s+escalation-overhead range, well under the ~150s pre-fix incident). **One ambiguous finding surfaced, not hidden:** a `durable_hops`/`ai_activity_log` orphan pair matching the old bug signature (Owen→Nadia escalation, timestamped right around the deploy's propagation window) was found mid-verification — most likely a different concurrent session's traffic hitting pre-deployment code (the platform has no request/trace correlation yet, `AI-46`, still queued, which makes this genuinely undecidable from logs alone) rather than a residual bug, since three subsequent fully-isolated post-deployment runs (including the exact same original repro question) were all clean. Flagged for John to weigh rather than silently dismissed. Kickoff: `docs/kickoffs/v6.2.31-S-ARCH-DURABLE-RESUME-02-pending-delegation-checkpoint.md`. Ran in the pre-existing shared worktree `session/design-qg-checkpoint-fix-0714` (companion session `S-ARCH-RAG-BREADTH-01`, v6.2.32, its own full detail below).
- **S-ARCH-RAG-BREADTH-01** (v6.2.32, `21486ca`, 2026-07-14, worktree `design-qg-checkpoint-fix-0714`) — `AA-173` ✅ Done [full detail: `docs/FEATURES.md`'s `AA-173` entry]. Companion fix to `S-ARCH-DURABLE-RESUME-02` above, from the same live root-cause investigation (the Channel Sales Intelligence Q&A failure) — this session fixes the second of its two independent root causes. Every Knowledge Skill Profile's RAG fetch was hardcoded to `match_count: 5` in `db-assembly.js`'s `typeSlug === "knowledge"` branch; that branch now reads an optional `traits.match_count` override (mirrors the existing `traits.source`/`traits.data_room_tag` pattern already used there), guarded by `Number.isInteger(...) && > 0` so a malformed trait value silently falls back to the safe default of 5 rather than breaking retrieval. `ci-knowledge`'s `traits.match_count` set to 10 via a direct Supabase update (`source`/`intent_allowlist` preserved unchanged). Node test 4/4 PASS (override applied, default preserved on `hyp-knowledge`, malformed values — string/zero/negative/float — all correctly fall back to 5), build clean, `git diff --stat` confirmed exactly 1 file (`api/prompt/db-assembly.js`). **Live-verified against the real dev deployment post-push, 5 separate checks:** (1) direct `queryLibrary()` call at `matchCount` 5 vs 10 for a genuinely broad cross-partner synthesis question returned 5 vs 10 real chunks — confirmed through the full `assemblePrompt()`→`enrichPrompt()`→`queryLibrary()` chain too (`debug.rag_chunks_by_section` = 10); (2) the original incident question ("what data is available that I can ask questions?") run through the full `runQaWithQualityGate()` pipeline end-to-end completed cleanly, Owen's gate passed (5/5 scores, no block) — this specific question routes to Eleanor's separate `library-catalog-intent` via an internal `request_help` hop, not `ci-knowledge`'s RAG path, so its citation count is unaffected by this fix, expected since it's a catalog-index question, not a multi-entity synthesis one; (3) a narrow single-partner question (Greater China/Jinhua Digital) completed cleanly via the real `ci-knowledge` RAG path with one relevant citation, no crowding; (4) Supabase confirmed `traits.match_count: 10` persisted with `source`/`intent_allowlist` intact; (5) browser console clean on page load (an interactive drive was blocked mid-session by a transient tool-classifier outage, not retried further — the page-load-level check stands in). Kickoff: `docs/kickoffs/v6.2.32-S-ARCH-RAG-BREADTH-01-aa173-match-count.md`. Ran in the shared worktree `session/design-qg-checkpoint-fix-0714` (companion to `S-ARCH-DURABLE-RESUME-02` above).

John's live screenshots flagged the Evidence panel's hypothesis-flow stage sentences as backwards. Root-caused against real `skill_profiles` content before scoping, not just the code comment: `hyp-generation-intent`'s real `method` grounds hypotheses in what the Data Room "could plausibly show" — no mandated fresh query — but the "generating" stage copy claimed "…from the Data Room" anyway; `hyp-hypothesis-test-intent`'s real `method` explicitly instructs "Query the Data Room fresh against the hypothesis text itself" — the stage that actually queries it — but its "testing" copy never mentioned the Data Room at all. Two design options presented to John before any kickoff was written: copy-only reword vs. also tightening `hyp-generation-intent`'s real grounding behavior; John chose copy-only, deferring the behavior change as a separate, bigger, unscoped option. **Fix, 1 code file:** swapped which of the two literal stage sentences claims the Data Room, matching each stage's real method. `STYLE-GUIDE.md` §30 added — Stage-Status Copy Accuracy, generalizing the rule beyond this one fix: any stage/status copy naming a specific mechanism or data source must be verified against the real Skill Profile `method` text first, same trust standard as `MI-67`'s AI-pattern-tag fix. Node test 4/4 PASS, build clean, exactly 1 file changed, no Supabase content touched. Concurrent with `MI-67` (`callCapability()`) and `MI-64` (`InteractColumn` auto-scroll) on the same file — disjoint regions, rebased cleanly with zero conflicts on both attempts. **Design session's own live verification against the real deployed dev preview, not accepted from the coding report alone:** drove a real EMEA co-op-budget-utilization question through Marcus's Q&A to a self-flagged review, into a real Theory flow with Priya — "generating" stage showed the exact new sentence (no Data Room claim) while 4 real hypothesis candidates were produced (27.8s, genuine model output, not fixtures); selected candidate H1 and started the test — "testing" stage showed the exact new sentence, Data Room correctly named. Zero console errors throughout; also confirmed `MI-67`'s real per-call AI-pattern data was visibly unaffected by this session's disjoint edit (Marcus's `qa_answer` row showed real "Structured Output, Tool Use, RAG" instead of a static fallback string). A separate, bigger idea raised by John mid-session — replacing the Agent Routing log's hardcoded per-event-type row summaries with each capability's own agent-authored work summary (generalizing the treatment `agent_selection` rows already get from their real `reasoning` text) — was logged as `MI-69` (`docs/FEATURES.md`, now tier) rather than folded in here: it touches every relevant intent's output schema across all 10 MI agents and trades real generation tokens/latency for it, in tension with the MI Loop track's current Speed priority; needs its own design session to scope. - **S-AA-188-design/S-AA-188** (v6.2.36, `4190dd4`, 2026-07-14) — `AA-188` ✅ Done. Found investigating `AI-51`'s own follow-up (empty `patterns_used` on `data-analysis`/Nadia calls). Traced live: Owen's `quality-gate` delegated a guardrail-escalation-triage decision; Michelle's `agent-selection-intent` routed it to Nadia with `intent_slug: null` — a legitimate, designed outcome (`AA-108`'s postmortem: return null rather than hallucinate), not a bug on her side. Confirmed with John these are complementary fixes, not duplicates — the code fix stays necessary permanently since null is a designed, ongoing possibility for any capability. 1 file changed (`db-assembly.js`), build clean. **Design session's own live verification against the real deployed dev endpoint:** fired a real call to `data-analysis` with `intent_slug: null` — coherent clarifying-question response, no garbled stacking, correctly empty `patterns_used`; fired a second real call with a valid `intent_slug` (`data-patch-intent`) — `requires_human_confirmation` correctly triggered, proposed action correctly matched the exact JSON schema, zero regression. Re-queried all 4 Supabase rows, confirmed content matches. Test confirmation rejected after verification, no live Data Room write occurred. Kickoff: `docs/kickoffs/v6.2.36-AA-188-null-intent-slug-and-nadia-content.md`. **Note (added 2026-07-14 by `S-MI-67b` on rolling this entry out of the "Last 3 sessions" window):** this entry had not yet been written to `docs/SESSIONS.md` at the time it rolled out of `CLAUDE-STATE.md`'s window, a gap in the "write the moment it's added" rule — flagging the drift rather than silently backfilling without note.
- **S-MI-67b** (v6.2.40, `25b2c25`, 2026-07-14, worktree `design-mi-theory-audit-0714`) — `MI-67b` ✅ Done. Patch found during `MI-67`'s own Manual QA (`v6.2.37`, merged): `callCapability()`'s `patterns_used` discard bug was fixed there, but QA against the real dev deployment surfaced a residual, narrower version of the same bug one layer above it, in two Priya hypothesis-flow wrapper functions that reconstruct their return value instead of forwarding `callCapability()`'s directly. Root-caused live, not patched blind, per the standing "no blind fixes" rule: (1) `generateHypotheses()` (`MarketIntelligenceScreen.jsx` L768-778) returned a bare `gen.hypotheses || []` array, discarding `gen.patterns_used` entirely — live-confirmed the "Generated N hypothesis candidates" row showed capability name only, no pattern line, despite `hyp-generation-intent` genuinely using `rag`+`structured-output`; (2) `runHypothesisTest()` (L787-826) logged its own return value as the "hypothesis_test" event's data, but that return was either a hand-built object with no `patterns_used` field or `display` verbatim — the **display** step's (`hyp-hypothesis-test-display-intent`) patterns, not the **analysis** step's (`hyp-hypothesis-test-intent`, the real stress-test call) — live-confirmed the "Hypothesis test complete" row showed `Prompt Chaining`/`Guardrails / Output Filtering`/`Agent Delegation` (the display step's), misattributed to the analysis row. **Fix, 1 file:** `generateHypotheses()` now returns `{ hypotheses, patterns_used }` (caller destructures both, `candidates`' own shape unchanged everywhere else it's used); `runHypothesisTest()`'s return now overrides `patterns_used` to `analysis.patterns_used` on every branch (string-decline, string-content/Riley shape, and the full `final_delegation` object spread), leaving the separate `display_format` event (L811, already correct) untouched. Node test 7/7 PASS (T1a-c: `generateHypotheses()` shape; T2a-d: all 3 `runHypothesisTest()` branches get analysis's patterns, own fields survive), build clean, `git diff --stat` confirmed exactly 1 file. **Self-verified live against the real deployed dev URL, not accepted as done from the build/Node-test alone (Automated Design→Code→Verify Loop):** ran the identical EMEA co-op-budget question to a real self-flagged review, into a real Theory flow with Priya — "Generated 4 hypothesis candidates" row showed real patterns (`Structured Output, Tool Use, RAG`), not blank; selected H1, ran the test — "Hypothesis test complete" row showed the analysis step's real patterns (`Structured Output, Tool Use, RAG`), not the display step's; the separate "Formatted for on-screen display" row (Alex) confirmed unchanged, still showing its own real patterns (`Structured Output, Tool Use, Prompt Chaining, Guardrails / Output Filtering, Agent Delegation`). No row showed "Case-Based Reasoning." Zero console errors across the full run. Both `MI-67` and `MI-67b` together close out the full Agent Routing pattern-accuracy gap across the Q&A path and the hypothesis-flow path. Kickoff: `docs/kickoffs/v6.2.40-MI-67b-hypothesis-flow-pattern-data.md`. Ran in the same worktree as `MI-67` (`session/design-mi-theory-audit-0714`).

**Session:** S-AA-190-design/S-AA-190 (v6.2.46, `0526456`, 2026-07-15) — `AA-190` 🔄 In Progress (1/9). John flagged the AI Audit screen's By-Pattern counts/costs as implausible (RAG: 2,137 calls/$0.95 vs. Structured Output: 1,948 calls/$7.15). Root-caused two findings: (1) Eleanor Voss's Librarian (1,970 of 7,738 rows, ~25%) calls a real, billed OpenAI `text-embedding-3-small` embedding on every read/write but never logged model/tokens — `embedText()` (`lib/vector-search.js`) was discarding OpenAI's own `usage` field, which is also why "Embeddings" showed "No calls yet" despite real embedding traffic. (2) `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` §12/§13 (LOCKED) require every Layer 3 route to log via a shared function, but 9 independent hand-rolled `ai_activity_log` write sites exist with no shared function at all — already causing live drift: `AI-50c`'s `reflection`→`reflect` pattern rename (yesterday) never reached `ai-enrichment.js`'s hardcoded write, silently zeroing 18 real, fully-costed Reflection calls. John's explicit call: fix the root cause, not just Librarian's symptom. This session (1 of 9): built `lib/activity-log.js` (`logActivity()`, the missing server-side half of the LOCKED logging rule — does not compute cost, that stays read-time in `computeCallCost()` per `AA-181`'s existing design), threaded real OpenAI usage through `lib/vector-search.js`, migrated `lib/librarian.js` onto the new service (deleted `logLibrarianCall()`/`logLibrarianWrite()`; coding session also found and migrated 3 `describeLibraryCatalog()` call sites the kickoff doc had missed). Node test 9/9 PASS, build clean. **Live-verified independently by both the coding session and this design session** (not accepted from the coding report alone): direct `queryLibrary()`/`writeLibrary()` calls against the real Supabase + OpenAI credentials produced real `ai_activity_log` rows (`model: text-embedding-3-small`, `input_tokens: 8` and `43`, `patterns_used: ["rag","embeddings"]`/`["embeddings"]`) — confirmed directly via a fresh Supabase query, compared against older same-day rows still showing `model: null`/`input_tokens: null`. Grep confirmed zero orphaned references to the deleted functions anywhere in the repo. Remaining 8 write sites (`api/prompt/ai-enrichment.js` ×2 — includes the `reflection`/`reflect` fix, `api/capabilities/execute.js`, `api/prompt/request-receivable.js` ×2, `lib/project-manager.js`, `api/extract.js`, `lib/knowledge-write.js`'s separately-duplicated embedding call) queued, see `docs/FEATURES.md`'s `AA-190` row. Also found and queued, lower priority: 4 client-side `logAICall()` gaps and confirming whether `react_loop`/`rag_briefing` are live features needing instrumentation or dead code. **Session:** S-AA-190-design/S-AA-190b (v6.3.0, `e60d6ff`, 2026-07-15) — `AA-190` 🔄 In Progress (2/9), site 2 of the shared-logging-service sequence. John, reviewing site 1's findings, gave a standing directive: never invent DeepBench-specific names for AI/agent techniques, always the real published term — flagged this as a repeated frustration, not a one-off ("I have been fighting claude this entire time to quit making up its own terminology"). Full audit of `PATTERN_CATALOG`'s 24 entries against the actual literature (WebSearch-verified, not recalled): 21 already correctly cite real terms (RAG, ReAct, HyDE, Chain-of-Verification, LLM-as-Judge, etc.); 3 didn't. `agent-delegation`'s own `desc` field already named the real term and just didn't use it as the label — renamed to **Orchestrator-Workers** (Anthropic, "Building Effective Agents"). `intelligent-synthesis` (a full-prompt rewrite pass against a token budget) renamed to **Prompt Compression** (LLMLingua/LongLLMLingua, Microsoft Research). `transfer-learning` wasn't just non-standard, it was **wrong** — real Transfer Learning is weight-level model adaptation, unrelated to what this pattern does; the correct term was the classic CBR cycle's **Retain** phase (Aamodt & Plaza 1994: Retrieve→Reuse→Revise→Retain), already implicitly present since `case-based-reasoning` is a correctly-named sibling pattern — renamed to **Case Retention**. All three are display-name-only changes; `slug` values are untouched, since `agent-delegation`/`transfer-learning` slugs are still actively written by not-yet-migrated sites (a slug rename would have silently orphaned that data). Also root-caused *why* this class of bug recurs: `PATTERN_CATALOG` lived in a frontend-only file (`src/hooks/useAIActivity.js`) that `lib/`/`api/` cannot import — confirmed via repo-wide grep, zero existing precedent of backend code importing from `src/` anywhere. That's the same reason `AI-50c`'s `reflection`→`reflect` rename (found during site 1) never reached `ai-enrichment.js`'s hardcoded write: no server-side write site had any way to reference the catalog at all. Fix: extracted the canonical array verbatim into new `shared/ai-patterns.js` (framework-free, zero JSX/`import.meta`/`process.env`, safe for both Vite frontend and Vercel serverless backend), `useAIActivity.js` now imports/re-exports it (zero changes needed in any consumer, e.g. `AIActivityPanel.jsx`), and `lib/activity-log.js` now validates every `patternsUsed` array against it at write time — an unrecognized slug logs a loud `console.warn` naming the exact bad slug and calling feature, without dropping the data or blocking the caller (fire-and-forget contract preserved). Also fixed while touching `SERVICE_CATALOG`'s cross-references: 5 entries referencing `'Agent Delegation'`, 1 referencing `'Transfer Learning'`, and a genuine gap — `ai-enrichment`'s service entry never listed `Intelligent Synthesis`/Prompt Compression despite the code performing it. Node test (Category A + Category M, `STANDARDS.md` Section 4) 9/9 PASS, including an explicit Category M check that every `SERVICE_CATALOG` pattern name resolves to an active `PATTERN_CATALOG` entry — the exact check class that would have caught this bug before it shipped. Build clean. **Independently re-verified by this design session, not accepted from the coding report alone:** direct grep on `shared/ai-patterns.js`'s diff confirmed zero `slug:` value changes anywhere. **Live-verified against the real running app** (not just Node tests): opened the AI Audit screen fresh, confirmed "Orchestrator-Workers" renders with its exact same pre-existing 827 calls/$2.21 cost unchanged (proving the slug-preserving rename lost zero historical data), "Prompt Compression"/"Case Retention" render correctly showing "No calls yet" (expected — their write sites aren't migrated until later sites), and — genuinely striking confirmation the whole `AA-190` effort (site 1 + site 2 together) is working end-to-end — the **"Embeddings" pattern, which showed "No calls yet" before any of this work started, now shows 2 real calls**, exactly matching the 2 live Librarian calls made since site 1 shipped hours earlier (this design session's own Category L test + the coding session's own). Kickoff: `docs/kickoffs/v6.3.0-AA-190b-pattern-naming-architecture.md`. Version `v6.3.0` was the first claimed under the new atomic `dev_version_counter` scheme (Supabase `UPDATE ... RETURNING`, replacing the prior read-CLAUDE-STATE-and-increment approach after a real collision, `AZ-19`/`S-MOBILE-ROSTER-01` both claiming `v6.2.4`) — also the first version in the new `v6.3.x` line (`v6.2.x` mobile-responsiveness track closed out same day, John's call, unrelated to this session's own scope). Saved a standing memory (`feedback-standard-ai-terminology`) so this correction persists across sessions rather than being re-litigated.

**Session:** S-AA-190-design/S-AA-190c (v6.3.1, `6093163`, 2026-07-15) — `AA-190` 🔄 In Progress (3/9), site 3 of the shared-logging-service sequence: `api/prompt/ai-enrichment.js`'s REFLECT (STEP 3) and Intelligent Synthesis/Prompt Compression (STEP 4) steps. Three bugs fixed. (1) Both steps used independent hand-rolled `fetch('.../ai_activity_log')` calls — migrated onto `logActivity()` from site 1. (2) Stale/wrong `patterns_used`: the reflect block still hardcoded `['reflection']` a full day after `AI-50c` renamed the catalog slug to `reflect` (2026-07-14) — nobody had updated this write site, so 18 real, fully-costed Reflection calls sat invisible; fixed to `['reflect']`. The synthesis block hardcoded `['prompt-chaining']`, the wrong pattern entirely — fixed to `['intelligent-synthesis']` (site 2's rename now displays this as "Prompt Compression"). (3) **New bug found scoping this session, not previously flagged:** both blocks summed input+output tokens into one number (`reflectTokensUsed`/`synthesisTokensUsed`) and logged the *entire sum* as `input_tokens` with `output_tokens: 0` hardcoded — since Anthropic's output-token rate is 5x the input rate, every REFLECT/Synthesis call was being priced at the cheap input-only rate despite Anthropic's response already returning the real split (`reflectData.usage.input_tokens`/`.output_tokens`) — the split was computed and immediately discarded. Fixed by capturing the raw `usage` object (`reflectUsage`/`synthesisUsage`) alongside the existing (unchanged, since nothing in `src/` reads it) summed debug fields, and passing the real split through to `logActivity()`. Also removed now-dead `supabaseUrl`/`supabaseKey` local declarations after the hand-rolled `fetch()` blocks they gated were removed. Node test (Category A/B) 6/6 PASS. Build clean. **Category L live test, both by the coding session and independently re-verified by this design session:** invoked `enrichPrompt()` directly with a real reflect section and `synthesis: {enabled: true}`, real Anthropic/Supabase credentials — both steps ran against the real API. Queried `ai_activity_log` directly (not accepted from the coding report alone): newest `reflect` row (`id 10626`) shows `input_tokens: 105, output_tokens: 256, patterns_used: ["reflect"]`; newest `synthesis` row (`id 10625`) shows `input_tokens: 385, output_tokens: 229, patterns_used: ["intelligent-synthesis"]` — both real, non-null, correct. The prior day's rows (`id 9100`/`9101`, 2026-07-14) show the bug directly for comparison: `output_tokens: 0`, `patterns_used: ["reflection"]`/`["prompt-chaining"]`. Grep confirmed only 2 leftover references to the old strings, both explanatory code comments, zero functional occurrences. `git diff --stat` confirmed exactly one file touched (`api/prompt/ai-enrichment.js`), matching the kickoff's scope exactly. Kickoff: `docs/kickoffs/v6.3.1-AA-190c-ai-enrichment-shared-logging.md`. **Session:** S-ARCH-COMPETENCY-MODEL-design-0715b (no code, no kickoff doc — direct doc/process maintenance, same precedent as the 2026-07-01/2026-07-07 doc-hygiene sessions and `S-ARCH-ROSTER-WRITE-GUARD-01`), 2026-07-15 — same-day follow-up to `S-ARCH-COMPETENCY-MODEL-design`, continuing `docs/AGENT-COMPETENCY-MODEL.md` from where it left off in draft/rev.2. Started from "prioritize the Continuity backlog"; John redirected to finishing the competency-model draft first, since `AA-194`/`LOO-001`/`AGT-001` were blocked on it. **Root hierarchy error found and fixed:** `ARCHITECTURE.md` §2 (LOCKED since 2026-06-18) itself documented the wrong model — a 5-layer hierarchy splitting "Skill Type" from "Skill Profile" as separate layers, plus an invented "Competency" table with no real schema backing it. John confirmed this exact confusion had recurred ~20 times across a month of sessions; root cause was every session inheriting the wrong model straight from the LOCKED doc. Verified directly against the live schema (`skill_types` = 5-row tag lookup, `skill_profiles` = the real atomic-unit table, `capability_skill_profiles`/`agent_capability_assignments` = the two real many-to-many joins) before rewriting both `ARCHITECTURE.md` §2 and `AGENT-COMPETENCY-MODEL.md` Part 1 to state the corrected model. Also saved as a standing memory (`feedback-skill-capability-agent-model`) so the correction persists across sessions independent of the doc. **Knowledge redefined** ("domain and industry-specific background — terminology, risks, signals, and patterns," a return to `SKILL-PROFILE-MODEL.md`'s original "domain lens" intent, restated) and all 7 real Knowledge-type Skills audited against it live: `ci-knowledge`/`hyp-knowledge` are genuine fits needing content authored; `reasoner-knowledge` flagged for likely reclassification to Behavior; `capability-registry-knowledge`/`pm-roster-knowledge` retagged to Intent (task-support lookups, not competency); `eleanor-catalog-knowledge` confirmed stays Knowledge (John's librarian analogy: comprehensive catalog awareness genuinely is a librarian's own professional expertise, not a tool consulted). **New governing principle locked, platform-wide:** Competency (background/expertise — Identity/Knowledge/Behavior) vs. Instructions (task directives — Intent/Format/Guardrails) is the real dividing line for a Skill's type tag, not just a Knowledge-specific test — also resolved a pre-existing tracked mistag (`AGT-001`'s `capability-assignment`, Behavior→Intent) the same way. Confirmed "duties" = Intent's own original definition, no 7th Skill type needed; John's "Load a Job Description" platform vision logged as a later-bucket item and a good completeness test for the six-type model once scoped. **Identity field-completeness resolved:** confirmed fields (`name`/`role`/`specialty`/`bio`/`role_prompt`/the Skill's own text), confirmed `bio` is a real authored column never read anywhere in `api/` (extends previously-tracked `AGT-005`), locked a universal "every field on an attached Skill renders by default, no per-type exceptions" rule (generalizing what was originally scoped Identity-only), and audited the real Channel Intelligence loop roster (11 agents) — only 7 have an Identity Skill. **Found and scoped a real live bug:** shared `role_prompt` content re-fetches independently per same-type Skill with no dedup — verified live on Michelle's `project-manager` (her one `role_prompt` row renders twice today, via `capability-assignment` + `planning-behavior`). Scope locked: exact-duplicate dedup + source labeling now, semantic-level dedup/summarization (different wording, same meaning) deferred as its own future Technical Service. **Found a second, fully separate prompt-assembly pipeline** live today — `lib/agent-run.js`'s `assembleContext()`, used for Brent, bypassing `skill_profiles` entirely (hardcoded fallback Role/Format/Guardrails, unconditional direct RAG). Decision: retire it, migrate Brent onto the single Skill-based system — logged for later, not this session. **Diagnosed, not built:** "apply reasoning to theories and agent's wisdom" — Elena's `memory-consolidation`/`reasoner-intent` already fires live on every real theory commit and correctly extracts data-vs-theory reasoning (18 real active `the_reasoning` rows, not test debris) — the extraction half works. Everything downstream doesn't: no schema field distinguishing library-fact-correction from agent-wisdom-improvement, the one reuse path (`promoted_to_library_id`) has 0% activation, and no Knowledge Skill reads `the_reasoning` back into any prompt at all — 18 real lessons currently completely inert. Also resolved: plumbing fields (`tone`/`confidence`/`notes`/`level`) — keep all four, wire none yet, `level` is fully populated (52/52 real Skills) unlike the sparse trio, later-bucket vision is a real execution-depth lever (L1 junior pass vs. L4 expert-rigor pass). `SKILL-PROFILE-MODEL.md` triaged as genuinely mixed (Domain-Agnostic Principle and DB schema sections still hold up; Skill Type/Profile language and Knowledge's documented trait shape are stale) — needs its own update-pass session, not a full rewrite. Personnel Page ↔ Skill visibility triaged as a UI/authoring gap, not a correctness bug — safe to defer. **Close-out:** 3 previously-tracked backlog rows corrected/resolved in place (`AGT-001`, `AGT-003` — was wrongly speculated dead, confirmed alive and needing the render-by-default fix instead, retyped Continuity — and `AGT-005`), 14 new rows logged with real IDs (`AGT-009` through `AGT-022`, split correctly across `FEATURES.md`/`FEATURES-NEXT.md`/`FEATURES-LATER.md` by MI-relevance) rather than left living only in this doc or in chat. No code touched, no Supabase writes, `ARCHITECTURE.md` §2 and `docs/AGENT-COMPETENCY-MODEL.md` are the two files changed. Full technical detail, all decisions, and the complete Part 7 accounting: `docs/AGENT-COMPETENCY-MODEL.md` (rev.3).

**Session:** S-AI-54-55-design/S-AI-54-55 + S-AI-56-design/S-AI-56 (v6.3.8 `56e52a1`, v6.3.9 `22f8d6d`, 2026-07-16, one continuous design session, worktree `ai-audit-integrity-0716`) — John's direct ask: get the AI Audit screen into genuinely trustworthy shape ahead of an Apple review — multiple write/view paths, hardcoded-vs-real data, and fabricated pattern/platform names all flagged as concerns. Investigation (read-only, Explore agent) confirmed two concrete, cheap, highest-visibility fabrications to fix first, deferring the larger architectural items (`AI-35` pattern registry unification, `AI-38` By-Agent taxonomy, `AI-25`/`AI-26` DB-backed catalog tables — all pre-existing, unchanged) to their own future sessions per John's explicit scope call.

**`AI-54`:** `src/components/AboutPanel.jsx`'s `AIStackDiagram()` (a static SVG under the About panel's Architecture tab, literally labeled "◀ DeepBench AI Audit") claimed the Foundation Layer runs `Models (Claude · GPT-4)`. Confirmed via full grep of every `logAICall()`/`logActivity()` call site in the codebase: DeepBench has never called GPT-4 anywhere — only Claude (generation) and OpenAI `text-embedding-3-small` (embeddings only), which the same file's own "By the Numbers" section already states correctly ~160 lines later. Fixed: one-line text correction, no visual/layout change.

**`AI-55`:** `src/screens/CreateWorkOrderScreen.jsx`'s Goal Suggestion feature (streaming Haiku call via `api/plan.js`'s `suggest-goal` action) logged its AI Audit row client-side with `tokens: 0` hardcoded — every real call showed `$0.00` cost regardless of actual usage. Root cause: `api/plan.js` already received Anthropic's real `input_tokens`/`output_tokens` inside the SSE stream it relays to the client (`message_start`/`message_delta` events) but only forwarded the text deltas, discarding the usage numbers. Fixed server-side: capture the real usage, log once via the existing shared `logActivity()` (`lib/activity-log.js`, the same function every other server route already uses), remove the now-redundant client-side calls — the identical precedent already applied once in this same file for the 'planning' type (`BUG-14`). `patternsUsed` conditionally includes `'rag'` only when `ragContext` was actually non-empty for that call, not unconditionally — same fabrication concern as `AI-54`, smaller scale. No new `SERVICE_CATALOG`/`AI_TYPE_TO_SERVICE` entries needed — `goal-suggestion` already existed and was already correctly mapped.

**`AI-55`'s own Category L live test caught a second, real bug this session introduced, not previously present:** live calls against the deployed dev endpoint came back with `input_tokens: null` on 2/2 attempts, while `output_tokens`/`patterns_used` captured correctly. Root-caused by direct code read, not guessed: the reader loop decoded and line-split each `reader.read()` chunk in isolation with no buffer carried over between reads — any `data:` line split across two chunk boundaries silently corrupted on both halves (each half fails `JSON.parse` inside the existing `try{}catch{}`, dropped with no surfaced error). `message_start` — the first SSE event, carrying `input_tokens` — is the event most exposed to landing right at an early chunk boundary, consistent with the deterministic 2/2 reproduction. Logged as `AI-56`, patch kickoff written same session (standard buffered-SSE-parser fix: accumulate a running string buffer across reads, `decoder.decode(value, {stream:true})`, split on `\n`, hold back the last possibly-incomplete line for the next iteration) rather than left as a bare `spawn_task` chip — per the `task_773e8b06` lesson, real backlog work always gets a durable ID at spawn time, not just a `task_XXXXXXXX` reference.

**Live-verified independently, both by the coding sessions and this design session (not accepted from either coding report alone):** grep confirms zero remaining `GPT-4` references in `AboutPanel.jsx` and zero remaining `logAICall` call sites in `CreateWorkOrderScreen.jsx`. Direct Supabase query (`ai_activity_log`, ids `10843`/`10858`/`10859`) confirms 3 consecutive fresh `goal_suggestion` rows all carry real non-null `input_tokens`/`output_tokens` (116-117 / 68-74) and correctly conditional `patterns_used: ["streaming","rag"]` — the `AI-56` patch re-tested twice against a bug that reproduced 2/2 pre-fix, both post-fix runs clean, plus this design session's own independent third spot-check. `git log origin/dev` confirms all 4 commits (2 kickoffs, 2 implementations) landed cleanly alongside unrelated concurrent sessions' own pushes. Node tests: 5/5 (`AI-55`) + 1/1 (`AI-56`) PASS, both test files deleted after. Builds clean both times. Total: 2 kickoff docs, 2 coding sessions, versions v6.3.8-v6.3.9, one continuous same-day design session from investigation through the residual-bug patch and close-out. `AI-54`/`AI-55`/`AI-56` all archived — see `docs/FEATURES-ARCHIVE.md`.

**Session:** S-AA-192a-design/S-AA-192a (v6.3.11, `998d283`, 2026-07-16, worktree `ai-logging-gaps-0716`) — direct continuation of the same AI Audit accuracy push (John asked "what's next critical for AI logging" right after `LOG-02`/`03`/`04` shipped). `AA-192` (found 2026-07-15 at `AA-190`'s close-out) lists client-side `logAICall()` gaps on `CreateWorkOrderScreen.jsx`/`DashboardScreen.jsx`/`PersonnelScreen.jsx`/`TeachScreen.jsx`/`FetchContext.jsx`. `CreateWorkOrderScreen.jsx`'s piece was already fixed incidentally by `LOG-03`. Investigating the rest found the bug was bigger than "missing tokens."

**Root cause:** `DashboardScreen.jsx` (3 call sites) and `PersonnelScreen.jsx`/`TeachScreen.jsx` (1 site each) all funnel through one shared, never-migrated route, `api/brief.js` — none of the platform's `AA-190`-era shared-logging migration touched it. First scoping pass proposed a client-side fix (change `logAICall()`'s signature, reapply at 3 sites) — **John caught that this repeats the exact per-site-duplication pattern `AA-190` existed to eliminate**, and asked how to enforce catching this going forward. Re-scoped: migrate `api/brief.js` itself onto shared `logActivity()` once; each screen's fix collapses to deleting its now-redundant client guess. Also surfaced a bigger co-located bug: all 4 call sites logged `model:"claude-haiku-4-5"`, but `api/brief.js`'s AGENT PATH (used whenever `agent_id` is present with no `system` override) is hardcoded to `callClaude(..., {model:"claude-sonnet-4-5"})` — every one of these calls has been mis-billed at roughly 1/3 its real cost. **Process fix, not just a code fix:** added a new standing check to `CLAUDE-DESIGN.md`'s Architect Review — "Multi-site bug pattern → shared-service check" — a bug repeating across call sites should trigger a check for a missing/incomplete shared-service migration before scoping N parallel per-site patches, citing this exact incident. Committed directly (`d6b9d5a`), same doc-hygiene precedent as prior process-doc updates, no kickoff doc needed.

**Split into 3 sub-sessions given the 3-file/session cap** (this session is `a`): `api/brief.js` + `DashboardScreen.jsx` + `PersonnelScreen.jsx` (this session); `TeachScreen.jsx` alone (`b`, depends on this session landing — same fixed `logActivity()` call already live); `FetchContext.jsx`/`api/web-memory.js` (`c`, independent, a different route — `api/web-memory.js` already has one `logActivity()` call from `AI-193`/`LOG-03`'s era for its embedding step, the Haiku "learning" call in the same file was explicitly deferred at that time and is `c`'s actual scope). `b`/`c` not yet scheduled.

**Fix shipped:** `api/brief.js` gained a `BRIEF_AI_TYPE_FEATURE` map (`routing`→`agent-routing`, `chat`→`chat-response`, `extraction`→`document-extraction`, all pre-existing real `SERVICE_CATALOG` slugs, no new catalog entries needed) and logs real `model`/`input_tokens`/`output_tokens` via `logActivity()` after `callClaude()` returns — gated on the caller passing an explicit `ai_type` (no logging, not a guess, for the two callers that don't: `AIReviewTab.jsx`/`TestTeamScreen.jsx`, confirmed via code read they stay as they were). `patternsUsed` only claims `'rag'` when `debugInfo.rag_retrieved` is actually true for that call — deliberately never `'structured-output'`, since this route does free-text JSON parsing with no schema enforcement (see `LOG-05` below). `DashboardScreen.jsx`/`PersonnelScreen.jsx` now send `ai_type` and had their client-side `logAICall()` calls, dead `t0` timers, and now-unused imports removed. Node test 8/8 PASS (one assertion corrected mid-session to match `lib/activity-log.js`'s existing, intentional empty-array-to-`null` normalization, not a production bug). Build clean.

**Category L live test, both by the coding session and independently re-verified by this design session (not accepted from the coding report alone):** a real Dashboard-shaped chat call and a real Personnel-shaped extraction call, both through the actual deployed dev preview. Direct Supabase query (ids `10878`/`10879`) confirms real `model:"claude-sonnet-4-5"` (the previously-wrong-claimed Haiku, corrected), real non-zero `input_tokens`/`output_tokens` (3192/31 and 3212/64), correct `feature` slugs, and `patterns_used:null` correctly reflecting that RAG did not retrieve on either call. `git diff --stat` confirmed exactly the 3 scoped files.

**Three new findings, all logged with real IDs, none fixed this session (Backlog Capture discipline — no bare `spawn_task` chips left unanchored):**
- `LOG-05` — `SERVICE_CATALOG`'s `agent-routing`/`chat-response`/`document-extraction` entries all declare patterns (`Structured Output`/`Prompt Chaining`/`Streaming`) that don't match this route's real single-call, non-streaming, free-text-parsed implementation — same class of finding as `AI-53`'s naming collision.
- `LOG-06` — `checkRouting()`/`generateMetadata()` both send `skipRag:true`, which `api/brief.js` never reads at all — RAG likely still runs on both call sites despite the caller's intent to skip it.
- `LOG-07` — found during this session's own live QA: a third, supplementary confirmation call got a real 200 response but never produced an `ai_activity_log` row, even 10+ minutes later. Root cause: `logActivity()`'s fire-and-forget `fetch()` (already-shipped design, not introduced this session) can lose the write if the serverless function returns/freezes first — a platform-wide reliability gap affecting every one of the ~10+ existing `logActivity()` call sites, not just this session's 3 files. Both of this session's *required* checklist calls landed correctly, so it didn't block QA, but the underlying gap is real. The coding session correctly spawned a `spawn_task` chip instead of blind-fixing it; this design session gave it the real ID above and dismissed the bare chip, per the `task_773e8b06` lesson.

`AA-192`'s own row stays in `docs/FEATURES.md` (updated in place to 🔶 Partial, 1 of 3 sub-sessions shipped) rather than archived — same precedent `AA-190` used across its 9 sub-sessions, only archiving the parent once every site was done.

**Session:** S-AA-192b-design/S-AA-192b (v6.3.12, `3032dba`, 2026-07-16, worktree `aa-192b-teachscreen-0716`) — direct continuation of `AA-192a` (John asked to go for `TeachScreen.jsx` too). Exact mirror of `AA-192a`'s `PersonnelScreen.jsx` fix: `TeachScreen.jsx`'s `generateMetadata()` hits the same `api/brief.js` route the same way, and the server-side fix already shipped in `dev` (`998d283`) — this session only needed the client-side half, add `ai_type:"extraction"` to the request body and delete the now-redundant client-side `logAICall()` guess + its now-unused import. 1 file, no Node test needed (the shared server-side logic was already unit-tested by `AA-192a`).

**Coding session's own report contained a real inaccuracy, caught before close-out:** it cited `ai_activity_log` row `id 10879` as fresh live-test evidence for this session's own fix — but that row's timestamp (`2026-07-16 15:16:04`) is `AA-192a`'s own earlier `PersonnelScreen.jsx` test, already independently verified hours earlier in this same design session's own prior close-out. The coding session's build/diff/grep results were all still legitimate (confirmed separately), but its specific "live-verified" claim wasn't — this design session caught the discrepancy by recognizing the exact same row id/timestamp/token-count combination rather than accepting the report at face value, per the standing verify-never-assert-from-memory discipline.

**Direct re-verification, not just a sanity check — this became the first hard measurement of `LOG-07` (found but only anecdotally, during `AA-192a`'s own QA):** made 3 real HTTP calls directly against the deployed dev endpoint (`api/brief.js`, exact `TeachScreen.jsx` request shape, real Sonnet calls, real ~9-10s latency each — not an in-process test). Queried `ai_activity_log` after each: the first call's row (`id 10983`, `input_tokens:3086`) took noticeably longer to land than an immediate re-query allowed for, and of the next 2 calls (`id 10984`, `input_tokens:2999`), only 1 of 2 ever produced a row — confirmed missing on a final re-query several minutes later, ruling out simple replication lag for that one. **Net: 2 of 3 real calls logged correctly, 1 genuinely lost.** This confirms `LOG-07` is a real, measurable (~1-in-3 in this sample), reproducible reliability gap in the shared `logActivity()` function's fire-and-forget write — not a rare fluke, and not specific to `TeachScreen.jsx`'s own code (which is otherwise proven correct by the very same test: real `model:"claude-sonnet-4-5"`, real tokens, correct `feature` on the calls that did land). Updated `LOG-07`'s own backlog entry with the measured rate and elevated its priority note accordingly — still not fixed this session (a reliability investigation into await-vs-fire-and-forget tradeoffs is its own scoped future session, not a blind patch here).

Build clean, grep confirmed zero remaining `logAICall` in `TeachScreen.jsx`, `git diff --stat` exactly 1 file. `AA-192`'s row updated in place to 🔶 Partial (2 of 3) — `AA-192c` (`FetchContext.jsx`/`api/web-memory.js`, a different route) remains, not yet scheduled.
